- **high-density-fight-prompt**: **只输出一样东西：最终的 MiniMax 提示词本体。** ([JGRFW/comfyui-AICG3D](https://github.com/JGRFW/comfyui-AICG3D/tree/HEAD/prompt_guides/H3%E9%AB%98%E5%AF%86%E5%BA%A6%E6%89%93%E6%96%97/high-density-fight-prompt) · ★641 · 2 copies)
- **high-density-fight-prompt**: 根据参考图和用户设定创作高密度、连续因果的电影级打斗视频提示词，并输出保持同一时间线的中文导演稿与 MiniMax H3 Ref2VA 英文六段稿。适用于 15 秒动作设计、武器战、徒手战、巨物战和参考图驱动的连续攻防。 ([JGRFW/comfyui-AICG3D](https://github.com/JGRFW/comfyui-AICG3D/tree/HEAD/skills/high-density-fight-prompt) · ★641 · 3 copies)
- **high-density-web-quality** ([iDorgham/Workspace-Factory](https://github.com/iDorgham/Workspace-Factory/tree/HEAD/factory/library/archive/legacy_pillars/10_engineering_devops/10_operations_qa/quality_assurance/skills/external/web-quality/high-density-web-quality) · ★2)
- **high-dim-intuition-rebuild**: Diagnoses where a learner's 3D geometric intuition is misleading them in a high-dimensional context (concentration of measure, Gaussian shells, distance-metric breakdown, manifold hypothesis, volume-in-corners, random-projection preservation), then surgically replaces the false picture with the correct one. Use when the user is reasoning about high-dim spaces (embeddings, latent vectors, neural net activations, large-scale data clouds, optimization landscapes) and either makes a claim that's true in 3D but false in 1000D, or expresses confusion at a high-dim phenomenon that "shouldn't" happen. ([lyndonkl/claude](https://github.com/lyndonkl/claude/tree/HEAD/skills/high-dim-intuition-rebuild) · ★200)
- **high-dimensional-data-clustering**: Use when binning assembled metagenomic contigs whose feature vectors are too high-dimensional for reliable Euclidean/Manhattan metrics — e.g. composition vectors built from multiple k-mer sizes — and hull-based assignment is preferred. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/high-dimensional-data-clustering))
- **high-dimensional-data-indexing**: Use when when you have millions of high-dimensional objects (e.g., MS/MS spectra converted to feature-hashed vectors) and need to compute pairwise similarities or retrieve nearest neighbors efficiently. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/high-dimensional-data-indexing) · 3 copies)
- **high-dimensional-data-reduction-visualization**: Use when a numeric feature matrix (e.g. 150 samples x 4 morphological iris measurements, or high-dimensional omics columns) should be projected onto principal components to identify unsupervised patterns and visualize sample structure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/high-dimensional-data-reduction-visualization))
- **high-dimensional-expression-matrix-neighborhood-search**: Use when a pre-processed, normalized scRNA-seq HVG expression matrix must be converted into a cell-to-cell graph (one node per cell, kNN edges) as input to graph-based contrastive learning or downstream imputation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/high-dimensional-expression-matrix-neighborhood-search))
- **high-dimensional-feature-screening**: Use when the feature matrix is high-dimensional relative to the sample size (e.g. hundreds of standardized OTUs vs tens of samples) and most taxa are expected to be noise, so the multivariate association test must operate on a selected subset S of taxa. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/high-dimensional-feature-screening))
- **high-dimensional-gene-expression-feature-selection**: Use when the task is selecting a small, sparse set of predictive genes from high-dimensional, correlated omics/expression data (p &gt;&gt; n) for regression or classification, and unknown feature groupings (e.g. ribosomal or MHCII genes) should be respected. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/high-dimensional-gene-expression-feature-selection))
- **high-dimensional-genomic-covariate-regression**: Use when fitting Cox or logistic regression with thousands of gene-expression/mutation covariates (e.g., TCGA mRNA Z-scores) and you need penalized variable selection, optional FDR screening, and cross-validated AUC/C-index. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/high-dimensional-genomic-covariate-regression))
- **high-dimensional-microarray-regression-benchmarking**: Use when a high-dimensional regression or classification task on correlated omics/microarray feature data needs a quantitative benchmark of sparse linear methods — e.g. reproducing the paper's Table 2 test-MSE and feature-count comparison across the five public microarray datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/high-dimensional-microarray-regression-benchmarking))
- **high-dimensional-mse-benchmarking**: Use when comparing prediction methods (e.g. SuffPCR vs sparse linear models vs non-sparse PCR) on high-dimensional omics data where both out-of-sample MSE and correct feature selection must be evaluated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/high-dimensional-mse-benchmarking))
- **high-dimensional-nested-cross-validation**: Use when a dataset has far more predictors than samples (P &gt;&gt; n, e.g. RNA-seq with &gt;16,000 genes in n=68 samples) and you need hyperparameter tuning plus an unbiased estimate of model accuracy or AUC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/high-dimensional-nested-cross-validation))
- **high-dimensional-noise-data-simulation**: Use when validating a cross-validation workflow for leakage bias: you need a null dataset (no true signal, P &gt;&gt; n) where any AUC above ~0.5 from filtered models demonstrates information leakage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/high-dimensional-noise-data-simulation))
- **high-dimensional-omics-cross-model-gene-intersection**: Use when fitting regression models with survival (or binary) outcomes against ~thousands to hundred-thousands of genomic covariates (e.g., 9,548 TCGA ovarian mRNA entries) and a robust, intersection-based candidate gene list with concordance estimates is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/high-dimensional-omics-cross-model-gene-intersection))
- **high-dimensional-omics-latent-embedding-generation**: Use when input is a high-dimensional protein x condition omics matrix (TSV/CSV, AnnData-compatible) with sparseness and redundancy, and a dense low-dimensional per-protein embedding is needed before network inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/high-dimensional-omics-latent-embedding-generation))
- **high-dimensional-predictor-ranking**: Use when a numeric predictor matrix x has far more columns than rows (transcriptomics-style P &gt;&gt; n) and you must rank/select predictors by univariate tests (t-test, Wilcoxon, ANOVA, correlation) before model fitting inside nested cross-validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/high-dimensional-predictor-ranking))
- **high-dimensional-rnaseq-predictive-modeling**: Use when the dataset has far more predictors than samples (P &gt;&gt; n, e.g. RNA-Seq like E-MTAB-11611), an unbiased estimate of model accuracy is required, and feature selection must be nested within CV to avoid leakage bias. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/high-dimensional-rnaseq-predictive-modeling))
- **high-dimensional-screening-benchmark-reproduction**: Use when you need to demonstrate or benchmark that marginal-correlation screening fails to recover predictive genes in a high-dimensional transcriptomic setting where p &gt;&gt; n (e.g., AML: 116 samples, 6283 genes). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/high-dimensional-screening-benchmark-reproduction))
- **high-dimensional-shape-similarity-measurement**: Use when base-level RNA-seq pileup matrices must be compared per sample against a gene mean shape reference, e.g. to derive per-gene on/off statistics or exclude genes with many off samples before shape-change analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/high-dimensional-shape-similarity-measurement))
- **high-dimensional-signal-enhancement**: Use when when you have raw or preprocessed mass spectrometry imaging (MSI) data with limited spatial resolution, high noise, or incomplete molecular coverage, and you want to enhance signal fidelity to support multiscale tissue–single-cell mapping or brain biochemical profiling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/high-dimensional-signal-enhancement) · 2 copies)
- **high-dimensional-similarity-search-optimization**: Use when when you have a large spectral library (thousands to millions of spectra) represented as high-dimensional vectors and need to match unknown query spectra against it, but exhaustive pairwise comparison is computationally prohibitive. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/high-dimensional-similarity-search-optimization))
- **high-dimensional-simulated-data-generation**: Use when benchmarking or stress-testing penalized regression (Ridge/Lasso/adaptive Lasso logistic or Cox) and you need synthetic n×p covariate grids with matching outcome columns instead of real patient data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/high-dimensional-simulated-data-generation))
- **high-dimensional-simulation-design**: Use when evaluating whether a sparse supervised dimension-reduction method still predicts well and recovers features when the marginal-correlation screening assumption is deliberately violated (zero-marginal-correlation, non-zero-coefficient features). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/high-dimensional-simulation-design))
- **high-dimensional-source-separation**: Use when dimensionality reductions of CLIP-based embedding spaces cluster images by artistic style rather than semantic class, and upstream PCA style debiasing plus contrastive debiasing have already been applied. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai/tree/HEAD/collections/agentic-ai/v1/leaves/high-dimensional-source-separation))
- **high-dimensional-statistics**: Sparsity, LASSO, compressed sensing, concentration inequalities, and covariance estimation. ([Ryukijano/agent-skills](https://github.com/Ryukijano/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/high-dimensional-statistics) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **high-dispersion-gene-selection**: Use when a raw single-cell count matrix must be reduced to a manageable, informative gene set (highest-dispersion genes) before autoencoder-based dropout imputation with AutoImpute. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/high-dispersion-gene-selection))

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/highly-variable-gene-selection--1d7d5ce7796406c3))
- **highly-variable-gene-selection**: Use when preparing raw scRNA-seq count matrices (e.g. h5ad from a multi-batch atlas) for integration or clustering and you need to restrict analysis to informative genes before embedding methods like scCobra, scVI, or Harmony. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/highly-variable-gene-selection--75df1e15ed287453))
- **highly-variable-gene-selection**: Use when a preprocessed scRNA-seq expression matrix (cells x genes, log2(x+1) normalized, genes in ≥3 cells retained) needs dimensionality reduction to a gene subset that preserves signal for rare cell type detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/highly-variable-gene-selection--62c7f9a377c1e08b))
- **highly-variable-gene-selection**: Use when a raw scRNA-Seq count matrix (thousands of genes) must be feature-reduced before AutoClass/control imputation or K-means clustering, e.g., top-200 genes for clustering evaluation or 1000/5000-gene subsets for the Tian et al. noise benchmark. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/highly-variable-gene-selection--f497e275809ac003))
- **highly-variable-gene-selection**: Use when preprocessing raw UMI count matrices (e.g., GSE115746 macaque retina bipolar cells) for DESC clustering, or whenever a compact set of informative genes is needed before deep-embedding or low-dimensional representation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/highly-variable-gene-selection--7378b9d6b0912bd6))
- **highly-variable-gene-selection-by-mean-dispersion**: Use when preparing a raw single-cell UMI count matrix (e.g. gene-cell-barcode matrix from Cell Ranger) for dimensionality reduction or clustering, and you need a compact set of informative genes. - **highly-variable-gene-selection-cite-seq**: Use when single-cell multi-omics data (e.g. CITE-seq PBMC or SMAGE-seq PBMC3K/PBMC10K) lacks spatial coordinates and you need pseudo-spatial locations for a spatially aware model like SMOPCA.
